和完整生成轨迹值得扫一遍，可以判断它的计算优势来自模型调用方式，还是来自较短输出长度假设。若关注大规模预训练，可重点追问三个问题：action vocabulary 如何影响 tokenizer 和 softmax；长文本下 action history 是否比 canvas 更难压缩；加入 Delete/Replace 后训练轨迹是否会变得不稳定。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether a language model can revise earlier parts of its output while retaining the simple compute profile of a decoder-only autoregressive Transformer. Standard left-to-right AR generation cannot directly edit previous clauses. Diffusion, insertion, and refinement-based methods offer more flexible generation orders, but often pay for it through repeated full-sequence passes, scoring all insertion slots, or long sampling chains.","importance":"The main value is in modeling design. Reviser keeps a next-token-like interface, but the token being predicted is an edit action rather than a token in final text order. This gives a lightweight alternative to diffusion LMs, masked diffusion LMs, and any-order generation schemes. The contribution is still exploratory: the formalism is clean and the compute argument is plausible, but the evaluation is limited to continuation, the current operator set is only Insert, Move, and Stop, and the paper does not yet establish large-scale pretraining behavior.","technical_approach":"Reviser maintains a mutable canvas and a cursor. At each decoding step, the model predicts exactly one action: Insert(token), Move(Δ), or Stop. The executor deterministically updates the canvas after each action. The Transformer attends to the action history, not directly to the canvas; the canvas is recovered from the history and used for validity masking. This avoids per-step scoring over all possible insertion locations and avoids iterative denoising over the full sequence. For prompt-conditioned continuation, the prefix is seeded through a deterministic insert-only action sequence, making the prefix visible in the action history while a validity mask prevents edits inside the prefix region. Training uses an obfuscation-restoration supervision scheme that converts target continuations into next-action prediction trajectories.","experiments":"The empirical section focuses on text continuation. The paper evaluates generation quality with GPT-2 Large evalPPL and pairwise arena judgments, and it analyzes compute with a FLOPs accounting. It reports that Reviser is strongly preferred over SEDD and MDLM in arena comparisons. Trajectory statistics show frequent backward moves and insertions into the middle of the canvas, indicating that the model is not merely imitating left-to-right decoding. The qualitative examples include a 300M Reviser trajectory where the model moves backward and inserts a clarifying phrase into an earlier sentence. The provided text does not include the full numerical tables, so exact win rates, evalPPL values, and ablation numbers should be checked in the experimental section.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 20–30 minutes. Read Sections 5 and 6 for the formal definition of the canvas, cursor actions, validity masking, and deterministic execution. Section 7 is important because the obfuscation-restoration trajectory construction determines the quality of the training signal. In Section 8, focus on arena results, trajectory statistics, steps per generated token, and FLOPs. The appendix material on compute accounting and full trajectories is worth checking if the goal is to assess whether the method has a real efficiency path. For pretraining researchers, the open questions are action-vocabulary design, whether long action histories become harder to model than final text, and whether destructive edits such as Delete or Replace can be trained without destabilizing the trajectory distribution.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"动作空间设计比结果数字更值得看；先读建模定义和轨迹统计，判断它是否真能避免扩散 LM 的全序列重算。","en":"The action space is more interesting than the headline numbers; read the model definition and trajectory stats to see whether it really avoids diffusion-style full-sequence recomputation."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Independent Researcher"],"authors":["Sean Diab"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["revision-capable-generation","autoregressive-actions","edit-based-language-modeling","non-monotonic-generation","decoder-only-transformer"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20830","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.20830","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.22005","title":"Abstention and Noise Filtering: Two Missing Primitives of Softmax Attention","abstract":"Gating the value pathway of attention reportedly improves language model pretraining, and prior studies disagree on why. We argue and provide experimental evidence that such gates supply two different things that softmax attention lacks: abstention and noise filtering. The first is abstention, which allows an attention head to output nothing, bypassing the requirement that attention weights must sum to one. The second is noise filtering, which allows the value pathway of an attention head to sup","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-18T17:03:58Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_22005.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"标准 softmax attention 缺少两个小但重要的能力：当没有内容可读时无法输出零值，以及 value 通路缺少按内容抑制干扰的非线性过滤机制。","importance":"这篇论文对 LLM 架构预训练有直接启发。它把近几年 value/output gating 带来的 loss 改善拆成两个机制：abstention 和 noise filtering。前者解决注意力权重必须归一化导致的“被迫读某处”问题，后者解决 residual stream 中特征叠加带来的 value read 干扰问题。对预训练研究来说，价值不在提出复杂新模块，而在给 attention gate 的收益来源做了可控拆解。这个拆解能帮助判断哪些 attention 改动会随模型规模继续有效，哪些只是小模型阶段的补偿机制。","technical_approach":"作者设计了匹配的 attention 变体来隔离机制。abstention 用 learned per-head sink logit 实现：为每个 head 增加一个 phantom key，它有可学习 logit，但 value 固定为零。这样 query 可以把概率质量分配给“什么也不读”，而不是被迫投到真实 token 上。noise filtering 用 value gate 实现，即在每个 value read 上乘一个门控信号。论文测试了两类 filter：一种基于 read norm 的阈值门控，另一种基于内容线性投影的 learned gate。为了区分“过滤”和“弃权”，作者还设置 routing-slot control：把同样的 gate 信号放进 softmax 内部，让注意力权重仍归一化。这个对照可以过滤路由偏好，但不能真正输出零值。这样的实验设计比直接比较 gated attention 和 vanilla attention 更干净，因为 value gate 同时可能承担零选项和内容过滤两种功能。","experiments":"实验覆盖 10M 到 350M 参数量的 matched models，并使用 paired seeds 控制随机性。主要结果有三点。第一，abstention 的收益随规模下降：在 10M 模型上，sink logit 几乎解释了 gated attention 的大部分收益；到 350M 时，filtering 成为主要收益来源。作者的解释是，大模型的 vanilla attention 更容易自发形成 attention sink，用某些位置，常见是首 token，来停放无用注意力质量，所以显式零选项的边际收益下降。第二，abstention 和 filtering 的收益通常可加；同时包含 sink logit 和 projection gate 的模型在所有测试规模上 loss 最低。第三，causal injection 实验显示，给 value read 注入噪声时，带 gate 的模型明显更稳，说明 gate 确实在抑制 value 通路干扰。不同 gate 也有不同盲点：基于范数的 filter 对沿范数决策变量伪装的干扰较弱，projection gate 对落在其判别方向之外的干扰较弱。论文还在附录中提供证据，说明这类 interference 在普通模型中实际存在，而不是只在人工注入设置中出现。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 30–45 分钟。优先看 Introduction 和 Mechanisms and Arms，理解作者如何把 value gating 拆成 zero option 与 filter。然后看 Section 4–6：Section 4 的规模趋势最关键，能判断哪类收益可能随模型放大保留；Section 5 看 additivity，确认两个机制不是同一现象的不同表述；Section 6 的 causal injection 适合关注实验构造，因为它比单看 pretraining loss 更能说明 gate 在处理哪类噪声。最后看 Section 7 的设计结论：sink logit + projection gate 是文中最稳的组合。若只关心架构落地，可重点看各 arm 的定义、参数/FLOPs 代价，以及 350M 规模上的 loss 差异。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Standard softmax attention lacks two useful primitives: it cannot abstain when no token should be read, and its value path has no content-dependent nonlinearity to suppress interference in value reads.","importance":"The paper is relevant to LLM pretraining architecture work because it gives a controlled explanation for why value or output gating often improves pretraining loss. It separates two effects that are usually entangled in a single gate: abstention and noise filtering. Abstention addresses the normalization constraint of softmax attention, which forces every head to put all mass somewhere. Filtering addresses interference from superposed residual-stream features. The main contribution is diagnostic rather than architectural complexity: it helps decide which attention modifications are likely to remain useful at larger scale and which mainly compensate for small-model limitations.","technical_approach":"The authors construct matched attention variants that isolate the two mechanisms. Abstention is implemented with a learned per-head sink logit: a phantom key with a learnable logit and a zero value, allowing the query to route probability mass to a true zero output. Noise filtering is implemented by gates on value reads. Two filters are tested: a norm-threshold gate and a learned linear projection gate over the value content. A routing-slot control applies the same gate signal inside the softmax while keeping attention weights normalized, so it can alter routing but cannot provide a genuine zero option. This design is cleaner than a direct gated-vs-vanilla comparison because a value gate can otherwise act both as a crude abstention mechanism and as a content filter.","experiments":"The experiments train matched models from 10M to 350M parameters with paired seeds. Three results matter. First, the benefit of abstention decreases with scale. At 10M parameters, the sink-logit mechanism explains nearly all of the gain, while at 350M parameters filtering accounts for most of it. The proposed explanation is that larger vanilla models learn attention sinks more effectively, often parking unwanted mass on special positions such as the first token, so an explicit zero option saves less loss. Second, abstention and filtering are mostly additive; the model with both a sink logit and a projection gate achieves the lowest loss at every tested scale. Third, causal injection experiments show that gated models are much more robust when noise is injected into value reads, supporting the claim that the gates remove interference. The experiments also reveal characteristic blind spots: each gate is weak against perturbations aligned with, or hidden from, its own decision variable.","reading_guide":"Skim time: about 30–45 minutes. Read the Introduction and the mechanism/arm definitions first; the key idea is the separation between a zero option and a value filter. Then focus on Sections 4–6. Section 4 gives the scaling trend and is the most useful part for pretraining architecture decisions. Section 5 establishes additivity between the two mechanisms. Section 6 is worth reading for the causal injection setup, which explains the gate behavior more directly than pretraining loss alone. Section 7 is useful for implementation: the sink-logit plus projection-gate design is the strongest and most robust arm in the paper.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看机制拆分和 scaling 图；350M 以下不能定架构结论，但“gate 不是一种东西”值得进 ablation 列表。","en":"Skim the mechanism split and scaling plots; sub-350M evidence is not decisive, but “gating is not one mechanism” belongs in the ablation queue."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Richard Zhe Wang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["attention","value-gating","abstention","noise-filtering","architecture-analysis"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22005","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.22005","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.20974","title":"Attention-Aware Routing: Coupling Routing and Attention in MoEs","abstract":"In Mixture-of-Experts language models, the router typically selects and weights experts based on the token's hidden state, utilizing limited contextual information. We propose Attention-Aware Routing (AAR), which augments the router with temporal and spectral features extracted from a sliding window of attention weights that represent a summary of the model's contextual state, disentangled from the hidden state. Keeping the base transformer entirely frozen, we train only the routing parameters, ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-17T18:29:05Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_20974.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"MoE 架构中的路由机制通常仅依赖 token 的隐藏状态（hidden state）进行专家选择，隐藏状态中内容信息与上下文关系信息相互纠缠，限制了路由决策的有效性。","importance":"探索了将注意力权重作为显式上下文信号引入 MoE 路由的潜力，揭示了路由与注意力机制之间的耦合关系，为改进 MoE 路由策略提供了新视角。","technical_approach":"提出注意力感知路由（Attention-Aware Routing, AAR）。在标准路由器之外，引入一个并行的路由模块，该模块接收一个滑动窗口内的近期注意力权重（可包含时域和频域特征）作为输入。通过冻结基础 Transformer 参数，仅训练路由参数，实现内容与上下文关系信息的解耦。最终的专家选择 logits 是标准路由器和 AAR 模块输出的加权组合。","experiments":"在 OLMoE 模型上，仅通过 SFT 训练路由参数，AAR 在 GSM8K 上比仅训练标准路由器的基线提升了 3.37 个百分点。分析表明，AAR 改变了生成行为（错误答案变短，正确答案不受影响），且路由变化会通过残差流传播，放大下一层的注意力 sink（attention sinks）。此外，AAR 在中深层网络中更为有效，早期层引入 AAR 可能会破坏事实检索。","reading_guide":"建议重点阅读 Method 部分 AAR 的具体实现（特别是注意力窗口特征的提取），以及 Introduction 中关于路由与注意力耦合关系的讨论。预计阅读时间 15 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Routing mechanisms in MoE architectures typically rely solely on the token's hidden state for expert selection, where content and contextual relational information are entangled, limiting the effectiveness of routing decisions.","importance":"Explores the potential of introducing attention weights as explicit contextual signals into MoE routing, revealing the coupling between routing and attention mechanisms, and providing a new perspective for improving MoE routing strategies.","technical_approach":"Proposes Attention-Aware Routing (AAR). Alongside the standard router, a parallel routing module is introduced, taking a sliding window of recent attention weights (which can include time and frequency domain features) as input. By freezing the base Transformer and training only the routing parameters, it disentangles content and contextual relational information. The final expert selection logits are a weighted combination of the standard router and AAR module outputs.","experiments":"On OLMoE, by training only the routing parameters via SFT, AAR improves GSM8K by +3.37 pp over a routing-only SFT baseline. Analysis shows AAR alters generation behavior (incorrect answers get shorter, correct answers are unaffected) and routing changes propagate through the residual stream to amplify attention sinks in the next layer. Furthermore, AAR is more effective in middle-to-deep layers; introducing it in early layers can disrupt factual retrieval.","reading_guide":"Focus on the implementation details of AAR in the Method section (especially the extraction of attention window features) and the discussion on the coupling between routing and attention in the Introduction. For a single token, participation is how many experts contribute knowledge to its output, execution is how many are actually computed (compute cost), and materialization is how many expert-sized parameter sets must be built and stored (memory cost). Sparse routing keeps execution and materialization low, but shrinks participation: for each token, only a few experts contribute. Dense output-m","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-18T06:01:13Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_21346.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"IntBMoE 解决的是 MoE 中三个量被耦合的问题：每个 token 能从多少专家受益，实际要执行多少专家，以及运行时要物化多少专家参数。现有稀疏路由、稠密输出混合、参数合并分别只能优化其中一部分。论文希望让全专家参与、稀疏执行、受控内存同时成立。","importance":"这篇论文对 LLM 预训练架构的启发主要在 MoE 设计空间。它把 MoE 的代价拆成 participation、execution、materialization 三个维度，比只讨论 top-k 激活专家数更清楚。对大规模 MoE 来说，瓶颈不只是 FLOPs，也包括路由粒度、专家组合参数的物化成本、以及非激活专家是否能得到学习信号。IntBMoE 的贡献是提出一种“先由全专家池合成少量可复用 block，再让 token 稀疏选择 block”的方案。它不是直接替代 Switch/GShard/DeepSeekMoE 这类 token-to-expert 路由，但提供了一个可分析的中间形态：全专家知识参与可以和稀疏 token 执行解耦。","technical_approach":"方法的核心是 block-level conditioning。模型维护一个小的 learned codebook，每个 code embedding 对应一个可复用 block。共享 hypernetwork 按层生成这些 block 的参数：对每一层，它根据 code embedding 将专家基底组合成一个 composed expert。这样，每个 block 的构造已经汇聚了完整专家池的信息；token 推理时不再直接路由到原始专家，而是由 router 选择少量 block 执行。结果是：participation 由组合时使用的完整专家池决定，execution 由 token 选择的 top-k block 数决定，materialization 由 codebook 大小决定。\n\n论文还引入 Dual-Path Residual Gating。它不是只把专家基底线性合并成一个单路径 FFN，而是分别构造 value path 和 gate path，再通过门控乘积形成非线性组合。这样可以提升专家组合的表达力，同时不增加原始专家池规模。和稀疏 MoE 相比，IntBMoE 避免了非选中专家完全不参与当前 token 输出的问题；和稠密 MoE 相比，它不需要对每个 token 执行所有专家；和 token/segment 级参数合并相比，它不需要为每个路由单元都生成一套独立专家参数。","experiments":"实验覆盖 ImageNet-1K、语言建模、序列推荐和线上推荐系统。论文报告在 ImageNet-1K 上相对代表性的稀疏 MoE 与稠密 MoE baseline 有稳定提升；语言建模和序列推荐结果用于验证该结构不只适用于视觉输入。工程侧结果更有信息量：IntBMoE 已部署到 AMap 的生成式推荐系统，在 60 ms 延迟约束下服务数亿用户，线上 A/B 得到 2.4% 相对 UVCTR 提升。这个结果说明 bounded materialization 不只是理论复杂度指标，在实际低延迟系统里会直接影响可部署性。精读时需要查看具体实验表，尤其是不同 codebook size、token top-k、DPRG 开关、专家数变化时的精度和延迟曲线。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 25–35 分钟。先看 Figure 1 和 introduction 中对 many expert-sized parameter sets must be materialized at runtime. Sparse routing, dense output mixing, and parameter merging each optimize only part of this trade-off. The paper aims to obtain full-pool participation, sparse execution, and bounded materialization at the same time.","importance":"The main value for LLM pretraining is architectural. The paper gives a useful decomposition of MoE cost into participation, execution, and materialization, which is more informative than counting activated experts alone. At large scale, MoE cost is not only FLOPs; routing granularity, parameter synthesis, and whether inactive experts receive learning signal also matter. IntBMoE proposes an intermediate design: compose a small set of reusable blocks from the full expert pool, then let tokens sparsely select among those blocks. This does not directly replace standard token-to-expert routing, but it expands the design space for MoE layers where full expert participation and sparse token computation are separated.","technical_approach":"$19","experiments":"The evaluation covers ImageNet-1K, language modeling, sequential recommendation, and a production recommendation system. The results suggest that reusing the same backbone over additional temporal steps can recover a large fraction of the gain from adding distinct layers, while using fewer parameters. This is relevant to compute allocation, latent reasoning tokens, and test-time computation. The evidence is still small-scale, so the result should be read as a mechanism study rather than a scaling claim for frontier models.","technical_approach":"The method starts from LRT, where the final hidden state of the previous token is fed into the current token through hidden-state residual feedback and projected KV feedback, controlled by learned gates and scales. The paper inserts a continuous latent thought position between two vocabulary tokens. This thought position has no discrete vocabulary identity. It takes the current token’s final state, runs it through the same backbone once more, predicts the next vocabulary token from the thought state, and passes that state forward through the recurrent pathway. The central comparison is an L-layer LRT with one thought token versus a 2L-layer LRT without thought tokens. Both execute 2L blocks per vocabulary token at decoding time, but the former shares parameters while the latter adds independently parameterized depth. The paper also compares against looped Transformers and PonderLM-2 to separate depth-wise reuse from temporal recurrent feedback.","experiments":"The main experiments use 16-layer and 20-layer NanoChat MoE backbones. With one thought token, the model recovers 67% and 81% of the BPB improvement obtained by doubling LRT depth, while using about 48% fewer total parameters. A second thought token improves performance further at both scales, although triple-depth LRTs still perform better under the same decoding block count. The key ablation removes the connection that feeds the final thought state into the next vocabulary token. This largely closes the gap with PonderLM-2, indicating that the gain is not only from another forward pass, but from extending the recurrent dependency chain across token boundaries. Equal block count does not imply identical system cost, since attention overhead and training details still matter.","reading_guide":"A 30–45 minute skim is enough. de","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-18T16:18:26Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_21960.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["math.ST","cs.LG","stat.ML"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究 factorization error。论文的核心问题就是把这个误差从“经验上存在”变成“可表示、可估计、可优化”的对象，并进一步回答：什么样的数据依赖结构下，schedule 优化只改常数；什么情况下，schedule 真的能改误差阶。","importance":"这篇对新型语言建模范式和采样理论都有启发，尤其适合关注 diffusion LM、masked LM、any-order 设计第一次有了比较干净的理论接口。贡献量级上，属于理论分析和采样设计上的扎实工作；如果你在做 diffusion LM 的训练—采样协同设计，这篇比单纯报 benchmark 的论文更有信息量。","technical_approach":"$1a","experiments":"从给出的全文片段看，论文既有理论结果，也有对 ρ 序列，而不只是合成分布。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a precise and important question in masked discrete diffusion sampling: why tau-leaping incurs error even with a perfect predictor, and how to choose the reverse-time schedule under a fixed budget of K sampling steps. In standard tau-leaping, multiple coordinates are revealed in parallel to reduce model calls. The approximation replaces the true joint conditional law of a revealed block with a product of one-coordinate conditionals. That creates an intrinsic factorization error whenever those coordinates remain conditionally dependent. The paper turns this from an informal observation into an analyzable object, and then asks the converse optimization question: for a given step budget, which schedule minimizes that intrinsic error?","importance":"This is useful for researchers working on diffusion LMs, masked generation, and non-AR sampling more broadly. method","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-18T14:28:28Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_21827.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是 明显下降。问题的根源是同一个概率分布被同时拿来做两件互相冲突的事：一是决定树怎么长，二是决定采样 token 在验证时应当按什么概率被接受。动态树想保留上下文自适应拓扑，随机采样又要求保留尾部分布，这两者在现有设计里打架。","importance":"这项工作的价值主要在推理系统，而不是预训练本身。但它对基座研究仍有启发，尤其是当训练目标和部署解码方式不一致时，系统层的概率语义会变得很关键。RheoSampling 说明，动态 speculative decoding 的瓶颈不只是工程实现，而是 dual-identity probability design。它在每轮动态树构建时，不再只用 top-k 确定性候选，而是在 top-m 的确定性候选之后，额外插入一个按真实 draft 分布随机采样得到的 token。关键创新在于，这个 sampled token 被赋予两种概率身份：在树构建阶段，它使用一个 proxy probability，与其他确定性候选竞争扩展和存活；在验证阶段，它恢复使用 true sampling probability，从而保证整体采样分布不失真。这样做的目的很明确：把“为了形成高质量动态树而需要的排序/竞争信号”和“为了保持 lossless stochastic decoding 而需要的真实概率语义”拆开。\n\n第二个技术点是 equivalence-class analysis。动态树一旦引入随机采样，树拓扑和 token identity 会耦合，直接证明 losslessness 很难。作者通过把复杂随机树空间压缩成若干等价类，给出可处理的理论分析框架，并据此证明该方法在 stochastic dynamic tree setting 下仍然 lossless。第三个点是 verification 侧的算法设计：作者引入基于 Optimal Transport 的验证算法，用来提高 acceptance rate，同时保持理论正确性。另一个配套优化是 sparse draft distribution，用来降低大词表上的采样与计算开销。整体上，这篇工作的结构很工整：先定位 one-hot dilemma，再用概率身份解耦解决建树与验证冲突，最后补上理论证明和高效实现。","experiments":"实验部分的主线是和 EAGLE-3 这类 top-k 动态树方法比较，在标准 stochastic decoding 下看 acceptance rate 和 wall-clock speedup。摘要和正文前半部分给出的结论是，RheoSampling 在多种模型和 benchmark 上都优于基线，且优势主要体现在温度大于 0 的真实采样场景，而不是 greedy setting。作者特别强调，EAGLE-3 在实现里会把 draft probability 视作 1.0 来做验证，这正是 one-hot collapse 的直接来源；RheoSampling 则通过 sampled token 的 true probability 保留了尾部分布，因此 acceptance rate 更高。\n\n从全文可见，实验不只是报速度，还在验证理论设计是否真的转化为系统收益：一方面比较不同温度下的 acceptance 行为，说明动态树若不处理概率语义，温度一上来就会明显退化；另一方面通过 OT-based verification 和 sparse draft distribution 展示额外收益。当前给到的正文片段没有完整数表，因此不适合编造具体百分比；但论文的实验重点很明确：在 stochastic decoding 下，RheoSampling 相比 top-k dynamic tree baseline 同时改善接受率和实际吞吐，而不是只在离线指标上占优。","reading_guide":"如果只是想判断这篇是否值得跟进，先看 Introduction 就够了，里面把 dynamic tree 在 T>0 时为什么退化讲得很清楚。接着看方法部分关于 dual-identity probability 的定义，这是整篇最关键的设计。然后看理论部分的 equivalence-class analysis，重点不是逐行推导，而是理解作者如何把“随机 token + 动态拓扑”的组合空间压缩成可证明的对象。最后看实验里的 acceptance rate 与 wall-clock speedup 图表，确认收益是否来自真实采样场景。\n\n这篇更适合带着问题泛读：如果你关心 speculative decoding 在产品里要支持 temperature sampling，而不是只跑 greedy，这篇有现实意义。若你主要做 pretrain，可把它当作 inference-time probability accounting 的案例，阅读 20–30 分钟足够抓住核心。若要深读，重点放在 losslessness 证明和 OT verification 的实现细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a practical failure mode in speculative decoding: dynamic-tree methods work well under greedy decoding, but under stochastic decoding (T > 0) their expand-then-rerank pipeline effectively collapses the draft distribution into one-hot probabilities during verification, which sharply reduces acceptance rate. The underlying issue is that the same probability object is being used for two incompatible purposes: building the tree topology and preserving the correct sampling semantics.","importance":"This is primarily an inference systems paper rather than a pretraining paper. A centroid base branch captures average relevance, while a rescu","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-17T18:28:29Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_20971.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"长上下文 LLM 推理中的 prefill 阶段计算成本过高，而现有的基于块稀疏（block-sparse）的 prefill 方法容易出现“均值稀释”（mean dilution）问题：即一个高度相关的 token 被大量不相关的 token 包围时，其所在的块的平均相关性得分会被拉低，导致该块被错误丢弃。","importance":"提出了一种无需训练的稀疏 prefill 方法，通过引入基于半径的“救援”机制，有效缓解了稀疏注意力中的均值稀释问题，在保持高稀疏度的同时提升了长上下文任务的准确率，对长文本推理优化有直接启发。","technical_approach":"提出 RBS-Attention，包含两个互补的选择分支：1) 基础分支（Base branch）：基于 query 和 key 块质心（centroid）的相似度进行打分，保留平均相关性高的块；2) 救援分支（Rescue branch）：计算 key 块的最大半径（即块内 token 到质心的最大距离），并结合当前 prompt、层和头的半径分布计算救援权重。半径越大的块（越分散），救援权重越高。两个分支独立设置阈值，最后将它们的掩码（mask）取并集，并结合 sink、局部窗口和最近块的掩码，生成最终的块稀疏掩码，交由 block-sparse FlashAttention 执行。","experiments":"在 Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct-2507-FP8 模型上，128K 上下文长度下，RBS 实现了 20.65 倍的独立 prefill 注意力加速，11.92 倍的 vLLM prefill 注意力加速，以及 5.97 倍的端到端首字延迟（TTFT）加速。在 Qwen3-32B 上，RULER 准确率为 88.65（稠密注意力为 89.52），LongBench-v2 得分为 0.376（稠密注意力为 0.394）。","reading_guide":"建议重点关注 Introduction 中对“均值稀释”现象的分析，以及 Method 部分双分支选择机制的具体设计（特别是救援权重的计算方式）。预计阅读时间 15 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The prefill stage in long-context LLM inference is computationally expensive. token。","technical_approach":"核心方法是 Predictive Multi-Tier Cache Optimization，简称 PMCO。系统在 prefill 之后、decoding 之前，根据已经产生的 hidden states 预测后续 cache 需求，然后联合决定哪些 token 保留 exact KV，哪些进入低秩 SVD 压缩，哪些 offload the KV-cache memory bottleneck for long-context LLM inference on mobile devices. KV cache grows linearly with sequence length and is accessed at every decoding step. This is especially costly on mobile SoCs, where usable DRAM and bandwidth are limited and shared with the operating system. The paper gives a concrete example: on a OnePlus 12 with 12 GB physical RAM, only about 6.8 GB is stable for applications, while Llama-3.2-3B already needs about 1.8 GB of KV cache at 32K tokens. Existing compression, eviction, and offloading methods reduce memory but introduce reconstruction cost, irreversible token loss, or I/O stalls.","importance":"This is primarily an inference-systems paper, but it carries useful implications for long-context pretraining and model design. A model's usable context length is not determined only by training sequence length; it is also constrained by KV representation, bandwidth, and cache layout at deployment time. TierKV treats KV management as a predictive resource-allocation problem before decoding starts. This suggests that layer-wise and token-wise KV importance is highly non-uniform. Architectures or training objectives that produce more predictable or more compressible KV states may translate into longer practical context on edge devices. The issue is even sharper for multimodal LLMs, where images, video, and audio can expand short user inputs into thousands of tokens.","technical_approach":"TierKV is built around Predictive Multi-Tier Cache Optimization, or PMCO. After prefill and before decoding, the runtime uses hidden states that have already been computed to forecast future cache demand. It then jointly decides which tokens should keep exact KV, which should be stored in low-rank SVD form, which should be offloaded to flash, and what SVD rank to use per layer. The optimization is constrained by memory and accuracy budgets and minimizes overlap-aware latency, so it accounts for whether computation, reconstruction, and I/O can hide each other. The predictor is entropy-guided and training-free, requiring no auxiliary model parameters or manually defined prompt classes. The execution engine supports heterogeneous attention layouts and avoids full-rank KV materialization on the critical path. It uses split-path kernels and overlaps flash reads with reconstruction to reduce stalls.","experiments":"The evaluation covers eight models across text, vision, and audio workloads on three mobile SoC families, including Adreno 750, Adreno 740, and Mali-G715. Baselines include llama.cpp, MNN-LLM, and MLC-LLM. TierKV improves prefill throughput by up to 1.6x over llama.cpp and up to 17.6x over MNN-LLM. Its fused kernel is 2.3x faster than naive SVD reconstruction, and the I/O scheduler reaches a 94.4% prefetch hit rate. It reduces RAM-resident KV-cache footprint by 12.5% to 34% and extends the maximum supported context length by up to 2.6x under the same memory budget. The paper also reports that naive SVD reconstruction on the OnePlus 12 adds about 30% average computation overhead as context length grows, which explains why compression alone is not sufficient.","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes 25 to 35 minutes. Start with the introduction and background to understand the mobile memory budget and why KV cache dominates. Then read the PMCO formulation, focusing on the joint choice of exact, compressed, and offloaded tiers as well as per-layer rank selection. The entropy-guided predictor is worth checking because it enables prompt-specific configuration without extra training. Finally, inspect the heterogeneous attention kernel and I/O-overlap results to see where the latency gains come from. For pretraining researchers, the most relevant parts are KV compressibility, layer-wise cache heterogeneity, and the deployment pressure created by multimodal token expansion.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"不是预训练论文，但把KV管理从‘边跑边救火’改成‘prefill后先规划’，这个视角值得看；先读方法图和主表，别急着信泛化。","en":"Not a pretraining paper, but the shift from reactive KV management to prefill-time planning is worth noting; read the method figure and main table first, and be cautious about generalization."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Zhihao Shu","Md Musfiqur Rahman Sanim","Jie Hu","Kun Yuan","Minghai Qin","Gagan Agrawal","Wei Niu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["kv-cache","long-context","on-device-llm","memory-tiering","inference-systems"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21172","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.21172","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.21858","title":"Watermarkable Multi-Draft Speculative Sampling via Poisson Processes","abstract":"Large language models (LLMs) have achieved state-of-the-art performance across a wide range of tasks, motivating two important aspects of deployment: inference efficiency and output provenance, which can be tackled by speculative sampling and watermarking, respectively. paper studies a deployment-side problem with a clean theoretical core: how to combine exact speculative sampling with detectable watermarking without sacrificing either output law or decoding efficiency. Prior work showed that this is difficult for a reason. Watermarking typically perturbs the sampling randomness or candidate set, while speculative decoding relies on a tight coupling between draft and target distributions to keep acceptance high. Once that coupling is disturbed, acceptance drops. The paper does not claim to remove this trade-off in the original setting. Instead, it improves the frontier along another axis: with multiple drafts and a Poisson-process-based exact list coupling, one can preserve unbiased sampling and recoverable watermark signals while achieving better efficiency than prior constructions.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense; it is mainly about inference and controlled generation. Still, it has two useful lessons for foundation-model researchers. First, many decoding-speed questions are really coupling-design questions. The acceptance rule is only the surface; the deeper issue is how to construct shared randomness so that exactness and efficiency coexist. Second, the watermark is integrated into the sampling mechanism rather than added as a post-hoc perturbation. That is a cleaner interface for provenance and may matter more broadly for controllable generation and verifiable sampling. In terms of contribution size, this looks like a solid algorithmic and theoretical advance within speculative decoding, not a change of modeling paradigm.","technical_approach":"$22","experiments":"The provided full-text excerpt does not include the full result tables, so only the main reported findings can be summarized. 类方法优化权重，SmoothQuant 类方法做通道缩放，QuaRot/SpinQuant 类方法做正交旋转。论文给出一个统一的局部误差分解，把这些操作的作用边界讲清楚。它的贡献类型偏分析和配置原则，不是单纯提出一个新的量化 recipe。对训练侧也有间接价值：如果某些激活异常结构会形成权重补偿无法消除的 orthogonal quantization，动态 scale 由每个 token 的 ||Z_t||_∞ 决定；若是 4-bit 激活，q_A = 2^{4-1}-1 = 7。权重量化器不限定为普通 rounding，可以是 GPTQ 等优化后的量化权重。\n\n核心分析是把局部 weight-activation quantization error 精确分解为两部分：activation-guided weight compensation 和 orthogonal residual。前者是权重量化优化可以沿着激活方向补偿的误差；后者与可补偿子空间正交，固定变换 P 后无法再靠权重优化消除。这个分解把“权重补偿”和“表示变换”分开了：前者负责在给定坐标系内拟合输出，后者负责改变残差结构。\n\n进一步分析 orthogonal residual 后，论文解释了三类常见设计。随机正交旋转可以摊平 quantization. Its central question is which part of the weight-activation quantization error can be compensated by optimizing quantized weights, and which part must be reduced by changing the activation coordinate system through rotations or scaling.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it is useful for base-model compression and deployment. Low-bit quantization failures are often attributed to activation outliers, while different methods address different pieces of the problem: GPTQ-style methods optimize weights, SmoothQuant-style methods rescale channels, and QuaRot or SpinQuant-style methods rotate activations. The paper provides a unified local error decomposition that clarifies the boundary between these operations. Its contribution is mainly analytical and prescriptive: it explains how to configure existing quantization tools rather than introducing a completely new quantizer. It also has an indirect training implication: if some activation-outlier structures create an orthogonal residual that weight compensation cannot remove, quantization-aware pretraining may need to control activation distributions, normalization behavior, and persistent channel outliers.","technical_approach":"$23","experiments":"The experiments evaluate backpropagation-free configurations guided by the analysis, rather than a newly trained quantization transform. The tested components include signed online Hadamard rotation, calibration-based sign selection, and L2 channel scaling. The paper evaluates W4A4 quantization across eight Llama and Mistral models and reports performance competitive with gradient-trained SpinQuant. The main empirical message is that rotation, sign selection, and scaling are complementary: they affect different structures inside the orthogonal residual. The concrete numerical details available from the text include the W4A4 setting, q_A = 7 for signed 4-bit per-token activation quantization, and coverage over eight Llama/Mistral models. Exact perplexity, zero-shot accuracy, and model-size-specific deltas should be read from the experimental tables.","reading_guide":"Skim in roughly 30–40 minutes. Its answer is no. A model can improve when every decision is conditioned on the reference interaction history, yet still fail when it must continue from its own previous actions. The gap comes from state drift, tool-use errors, missing information, and error propagation across turns.","importance":"This is mainly an evaluation and diagnosis paper, not a pretraining method paper. Its value for LLM training is in the interface between SFT/RL and agent evaluation. It shows that next-action imitation metrics can be misleading for long-horizon tool agents. If training and validation only measure local decisions under gold histories, they may reward policies that look competent turn by turn but cannot maintain a valid interaction state. For agent post-training, the implication is to treat trajectory success, tool-specific behavior, and error propagation as first-class metrics.","technical_approach":"$25","experiments":"SFT consistently improves text quality and overall next-turn success under gold-history evaluation. This holds across the tested Qwen3 and Gemma model sizes. The improvement does not carry over to autonomous workflow execution. Trajectories requiring tool use almost always fail, and the SFT models do not achieve holistic workflow success in a way that matches their turn-level gains. The tool-specific analysis also shows that aggregate next-turn scores can hide capability mismatch: better natural-language responses do not imply reliable tool calls, and local tool improvements do not guarantee trajectory completion. The qualitative trajectory analysis is important because it identifies the failure mechanism: once an early decision changes the state, later decisions are made under a self-induced history that the gold-history protocol never tests.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 15–20 minutes. tokenization、长上下文压缩和事件级表示的短板。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Existing video understanding benchmarks mostly test single-step scene recognition or global summarization, and they do not adequately evaluate whether MLLM agents can perform multi-hop spatial, temporal, and causal reasoning over long videos.","importance":"This is primarily an evaluation contribution. Its direct impact on foundation-model pretraining is limited, but it is useful for multimodal agents and long-video reasoning evaluation. The benchmark shifts video QA toward active evidence seeking: a model must inspect relevant time windows, regions, and event chains rather than rely on uniformly sampled frames. For pretraining researchers, the signal is that current video MLLMs still struggle with long-range event dependencies and that future video pretraining or mid-training data may need explicit multi-step evidence traces, not only video-caption pairs or short-form QA.","technical_approach":"AgentVidBench contains 100 complex multiple-choice questions over 71 public videos, with durations up to 30 minutes. The authors prohibit questions that can be answered from a single frame or a single localized timestamp, forcing compositional evidence gathering. Each question has 26 answer choices, labeled A–Z, which lowers random-guess accuracy to 3.85%. The videos span 12 fine-grained genres grouped into 4 super-categories. Each item is cross-validated by multiple human annotators to ensure one correct answer. A key design choice is to provide step-by-step solution traces for every question, enabling evaluation of agent trajectories rather than only final answers. The evaluation includes final accuracy and a trajectory score, and the paper also proposes a simple active video-inspection workflow for controllable spatial, temporal, and causal navigation.","experiments":"The paper evaluates several strong MLLMs and their agentic variants, including GPT-5, Claude Opus 4.7, Gemini 2.5, Qwen3-VL, Qwen3.5, Gemma 4, and compatible agent workflows such as STAR, DVD, and AVP. The reported pattern is that single-turn MLLMs struggle on AgentVidBench, while agentic workflows improve both final accuracy and trajectory scores. Ablations indicate that adaptive spatial inspection, temporal localization, and causal backtracking or forward tracing are useful for complex video QA. Existing benchmarks often rely on question answering or ground-truth caption matching, where models may succeed through superficial cues and incomplete annotations. To this end, we introduce VidOmni-Bench, a benchmark that requires models to verify whether each event in dense video captions is supported by the video. VidOmni","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-18T09:09:34Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_21521.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是视频大模型评测失真问题。现有视频 caption，模型要逐句判断每个事件描述是否被视频支持。这样评测目标从“会不会生成像样答案”转成“能不能核验事件是否真实发生、是否在正确时间发生”。","importance":"对预训练本身不是直接方法贡献，但对视频-语言模型和原生多模态基座模型的评测很有参考价值。它的价值在于把评测对象从粗粒度 QA 正确率推进到事件级 grounded verification，并且显式拆开两个变量：视频时空复杂度和视频时长。这样可以更清楚地看到模型到底是败在长时依赖、细粒度动作识别、场景切换，还是 hallucination 检测能力不足。对做多模态 pretrain 的人，这类 benchmark 能帮助判断训练目标是否真的学到了时序 grounding，而不是只学会了 caption 4 秒到 90 分钟。作者刻意把“复杂度”和“时长”作为两条独立轴来采样，这一点比很多混在一起的长视频 benchmark 更干净。\n\n标注流程也有设计。作者不依赖人工写 ground-truth dense captions，而是先用五个不同 Video-LLM 生成 dense captions，包括 InternVL3.5、Qwen3-VL、video-SALMONN 2+、Keye-VL 1.5 和 Gemini 3 Flash，共得到 2500 个 video-caption 对。随后做人类两阶段标注，对 caption 逐句核验，给出句级二分类标签。模型生成中那些“看起来合理但实际上不被视频支持”的句子，被保留下来作为 hard negatives。这个构造方式有两个好处：一是负例更贴近真实模型 hallucination，而不是人工编造的明显错误；二是 caption 覆盖更密，能逼近长视频中的事件流。\n\n从评测设计上看，这个 benchmark 不是在测开放式生成文风，而是在测 grounded verification 能力。它要求模型检查对象状态、动作、背景变化、场景切换以及时间一致性，因此比普通 QA 更接近“视频证据审查”。","experiments":"实验结论很明确：当前 Video-LLM 在 VidOmni-Bench 个带音频，说明部分样本可能涉及音视频联合线索。当前提供的正文片段没有完整结果表和具体分数，因此不适合编造数值；但可以确定的是，作者的重点不是比较谁分数最高，而是用分层分析揭示：长时视频、复杂交互和多场景切换会显著放大模型的事件核验困难。","reading_guide":"这是典型的评测论文，泛读即可。先看任务定义和 Figure 3，理解 sentence-wise verification The sheer breadth and diversity of the tasks humans can perform and think about pose a challenge for scalable and rigorous comparison between humans and models. Standard LLM benchmarks usually measure correctness against an answer key, while cognitive experiments measure response patterns under controlled manipulations: biases, graded judgments, probabilistic choices, and individual differences. This gives a more diagnostic view of whether a pretrained model matches human-like behavioral structure, rather than only solving benchmark items. It can also expose how pretraining mixtures, instruction tuning, and reasoning-oriented post-training change model behavior across classical cognitive effects.","technical_approach":"The main technical object is an Experiment Markup Language, or EML, used to standardize diverse cognitive experiments into a task-agnostic executable representation. The source experiments may come from PsychoPy, jsPsych, MATLAB, psiTurk, or custom platforms, with different stimulus formats, trial structures, instructions, and response protocols. CogGym uses a semi-automated pipeline with human validation to preserve the original experimental design. This is the key distinction from prompt-only conversions: the framework tries to match instructions, stimuli, trial order, response format, and published human response patterns, reducing the risk that observed model-human gaps are artifacts of evaluation translation.","experiments":"The provided full text mainly contains motivation and framework description; it does not include complete numerical results, task counts, model lists, or measured human-model alignment scores. What can be inferred is that the intended scope includes text, image, and video paradigms, with comparisons performed at the matched-trial level. In a complete version, the most useful results would be whether converted EML experiments reproduce known human effects, how different models correlate with human response distributions, and where models systematically diverge across cognitive domains and modalities.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 15 to 25 minutes. Benchmarks for agentic code generation check correctness with held-out test suites, which are inherently incomplete and increasingly susceptible to memorization. Formal verification avoids both problems, but existing work covers only standalone tasks whose specifications are given as input, not real issues, which touch large repositories and state intent in vague natural language. We present Bench","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-18T01:16:53Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2609_21190.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.SE"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的是：能不能把真实软件仓库中的自然语言 issue，转成带机器检查证明的 coding benchmark，从而用“形式验证”替代不完备的隐藏测试集。问题的关键不只是给代码加证明，而是如何在大代码库、模糊需求、跨文件依赖的现实条件下，构造一个可操作的验证 oracle。已有 verified code generation 多半针对小型独立题目，且把形式化规格当输入；SWE-Proof 面对的是 SWE-bench 这类真实仓库问题，规格并不存在，需要从 ground-truth patch 反推出可验证的 formal spec，并处理未修改依赖函数的语义摘要。","importance":"对 LLM 基座研究的直接价值不在于预训练方法本身，而在于评测与训练信号质量。coding agent 近两年大量依赖 hidden tests 作为 reward 和评测，但测试不完备、容易被 reward hacking，也越来越容易被记忆污染。SWE-Proof 给出的结论很清楚：即便通过所有隐藏测试，仍有 1/4 到 1/2 的补丁是错的；而一旦提供正确形式规格，前沿模型的解决率可以从 80% 出头抬到 95% 左右。也就是说，当前瓶颈不是“模型不会修代码”，而是“规格不够准，验证信号不够硬”。这对 RL for code、合成 verifier、以及更一般的 post-train reward design 都有很强启发。它把问题从“如何生成 patch”重新拉回“如何生成 faithful specification”。","technical_approach":"$28","experiments":"数据集方面，SWE-Proof 覆盖了 SWE-bench Verified 的全部 500 个实例，并扩展到 SWE-bench Pro 的 Python 子集。每个实例都带有 ground-truth formal specification、实现和 correctness proof，支持三种验证后端。这个规模在“真实仓库 issue + 形式验证”设定下已经相当可观。\n\n模型评测使用两款前沿模型：Claude Opus 4.8 和 GPT-5.5。最关键的发现有几条。第一，通过所有隐藏测试并不等于正确：有 25% 到 50% 的 patch 仍然存在问题。第二，给一个结构化自然语言“规格”并不能显著弥合这个差距，说明非形式化约束仍然太松。第三，让 agent 自己写 specification 再做验证，并没有比无辅助基线更好；但如果直接提供正确 formal specification，性能会明显提升。具体数字上，Opus 4.8 的 resolution 从 85.0% 提升到 95.1%，GPT-5.5 从 81.2% 提升到 94.7%。这说明当前模型在“按准规格实现”上已经不差，真正卡住的是“把模糊需求写成 faithful formal spec”。\n\n规格合成质量分析是全文最值得看的实验。作者报告只有 62% 的合成规格能通过 adversarial audit，主要失败模式是 faithfulness 不足。更细一点，未解决实例中有 89% 的规格未通过审计，而已解决实例中这一比例是 47%。这说明 specification quality 与最终解题成功高度相关，不是边缘因素。\n\n如果从 reward design 角度看，这些结果也意味着：形式验证并不会自动让 agent 更强，除非规格本身足够好。错误规格会把验证从“强监督”变成“错误监督”，甚至比弱测试更危险，因为它给出的是看似严格的错误信号。","reading_guide":"这篇虽然标成 skim，但如果你关心 coding RL 的 verifier 质量，值得读得比 skim 稍深。先看 Introduction，作者把 hidden tests 的两个核心问题讲得很清楚：不完备性和可被利用性。然后直接看 Overview/Figure 1，理解一个 SWE-bench issue 是如何被转成 formal spec、callee axioms、以及后端可验证实现的。只看摘要很容易以为这是“给 SWE-bench 加证明”，但全文真正有意思的是如何控制验证边界。\n\n方法部分重点看 Benchproofer 的两件事：一是 environment axiomatization，二是 mechanical + adversarial gates。前者决定这件事能否扩展到真实仓库，后者决定 benchmark 质量是否可信。实验部分优先看四类结果：test-passing 但错误的比例；给正确 formal spec 前后的性能差；spec synthesis 的 audit 通过率；以及失败案例里 faithfulness failure 的具体形态。\n\n如果时间有限，建议读 Introduction、Overview、方法里关于 axiomatization 和 admission gates 的小节，再看主结果表和 specification failure analysis，40 分钟左右能抓住主线。深挖时可以重点想三个问题：axioms 是否会引入过强假设；faithfulness audit 的可靠性如何；以及这类 formal oracle 能否反过来用于 RL 训练，而不只是 benchmark。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether real-world software issues can be turned into coding tasks with machine-checked correctness, replacing hidden test suites with formal verification. The challenge is not just proving code. It is building a usable verification oracle for realistic repository-level tasks, where intent is expressed in vague natural language, fixes touch large codebases, and formal specifications do not exist upfront. Existing verified code generation mostly targets small standalone problems and assumes the formal spec is given. SWE-Proof instead starts from SWE-bench-style issues and constructs the spec, implementation, and proof artifacts needed for verification.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model research is mainly through evaluation and reward quality. Coding agents are still trained and evaluated largely with hidden tests, but tests are incomplete, vulnerable to reward hacking, and increasingly exposed to memorization. SWE-Proof makes the gap concrete: between one quarter and one half of patches that pass all hidden tests are still flawed. At the same time, when a correct formal specification is provided, frontier models jump from the low-80s or mid-80s to roughly 95% resolution. That shifts the bottleneck. The main problem is no longer only patch synthesis; it is faithful specification synthesis. Consequently, they inherently incur excessive linguistic overhead, leading to information dilution and weak visual grounding. To address this challenge, we propose Dense Reasoning Trace (DRT), a paradigm that departs from natural-language-centered CoT by expressing reasoning as compact structured traces, which include c","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-18T12:09:58Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2609_21675.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的是多模态 CoT 的表达空间问题。作者认为，当前 MLLM 的推理轨迹主要写在自然语言里，导致两个直接后果：一是 token 冗余很高，推理成本大；二是随着轨迹变长，模型越来越容易盯着自己生成的文字，而不是回到图像证据，造成视觉 grounding 变弱。论文的核心问题不是单纯“怎么少输出一点 token”，而是“能不能把推理从自然语言表达空间迁到更紧凑、更结构化的 trace 空间，同时保持甚至提升正确性和 grounding”。","importance":"从基座预训练视角看，这不是 pretrain 论文，而是 post-train / reasoning-format 方向的工作，但它对训练信号设计有现实启发。它提示一个重要点：推理能力不只取决于模型参数和 test-time compute，也取决于中间轨迹的表示形式。若中间表示过于语言化，模型会把大量容量花在表述而不是推理本身。对后训练研究，这篇工作的价值在于把“压缩 CoT”从简单的长度控制，推进到“重写 reasoning language”——用结构化 trace 替代自然语言长链条，并且用 RL 去约束逻辑一致性和视觉忠实性。贡献量级中等，偏方法工程与训练框架整合，对预训练本身的直接影响有限，但对 pretrain↔post-train 接口、尤其是 reasoning data format 的设计有参考意义。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 Dense Reasoning Trace，简称 DRT。它不是让模型少说话，而是让模型换一种方式“思考并表达”。DRT 把推理拆成两个显式部分：<visual> 里放视觉观察到的证据，<think> 里放紧凑的逻辑推导；中间用符号化连接词和简短状态表示，而不是自然语言长句。这样做的目的有两个：减少语言表述冗余；把“看到了什么”和“由此推出什么”分开，降低把假设写成观察事实的风险。\n\n训练分两阶段。第一阶段是 Dense Trace Initialization。作者把高质量多模态 instruction 数据重写成 DRT 格式，构造 DRT-SFT 数据集，用监督微调让模型先学会这种新的 reasoning mode。这里关键不是知识注入，而是格式内化：模型要习惯在紧凑结构里组织中间状态。\n\n第二阶段是 Trace-Grounded Reinforcement Learning。作者构造 DRT-RL 数据集，使用 tri-perspective verification 过滤轨迹，只保留同时满足三项条件的样本：最终答案正确、中间步骤逻辑一致、视觉 grounding 忠实。然后在这些参考轨迹上做 Trace-Grounded GRPO，并结合 process-based rewards 和 exploratory incentives。可以理解为：不仅奖励答对，还奖励推理链条短、逻辑通、且每一步没有脱离视觉证据。\n\n和已有“省 token”方法相比，区别在于它不是训练时学一个 budget predictor，也不是推理时做 early stop 或 salient token selection，而是直接改变 reasoning trace 的表示语言。和普通 CoT SFT/RL 相比，它额外强调 observation 与 deduction 的解耦，以及对中间过程的 grounding 验证。","experiments":"作者在五个有挑战的多模态推理 benchmark 上评估，包括文本任务和 out-of-distribution 视频任务。主结果是：相对 Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct 的官方 prompting，DRT 方案实现了 5.5 倍 token 效率提升，同时准确率提升 1.3 个点。这个结果说明它不是单纯用更短输出换更差性能，而是在更紧预算下保持甚至略增准确率。\n\n从论文叙述看，实验重点不只是最终分数，还包括效率-准确率 tradeoff 和各组件消融。Dense Trace Initialization 负责把模型拉进新的表达空间；如果没有这一步，模型即使被要求简写，也未必能稳定地产生高密度 trace。Trace-Grounded RL 则进一步改善 reasoning faithfulness，尤其是在 token budget 紧的时候，能减少为了压缩而牺牲逻辑完整性或视觉 grounding 的情况。tri-perspective verification 的作用是提高 RL 参考轨迹质量，避免把答案对但过程乱、或过程看似合理但不 grounded 的样本喂给策略优化。\n\n不过，从基座研究角度也要看到边界。第一，收益主要建立在特定 trace schema 和重写数据流程上，泛化到别的模型家族、别的模态比例、或纯文本 reasoning 是否同样成立，还需要更多证据。第二，5.5× token efficiency 的定义依赖输出长度和任务设置，阅读时要仔细看是否包含输入 token、是否在统一 prompt 模板下比较。第三，这类结构化 trace 可能把一部分推理负担转移到 schema 设计和数据重写质量上，因此数据构造细节很关键。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议先看 Figure 1 和方法总览，先弄清 DRT 到底长什么样，别把它理解成普通的 concise CoT。然后看两阶段训练：SFT 重写数据怎么构造，RL 的 verification 和 reward 怎么定义。最后看主表里 token 使用量、准确率、以及效率-准确率曲线，确认收益来自表达空间变化，而不只是 prompt 技巧。\n\n如果时间有限，读 Introduction、DRT representation、Dense Trace Initialization、Trace-Grounded RL 和主实验即可，预计 25 分钟左右能抓住主要贡献。若要深挖，重点看三处：1）DRT-SFT 数据重写规则，决定 trace 是否真的保留关键信息；2）tri-perspective verification 的判定标准，决定 RL 信号质量；3）消融实验里 observation/deduction 解耦、process reward、exploration incentive 各自带来的增益。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a representation problem in multimodal reasoning. The authors argue that current MLLM chain-of-thought operates in natural language, which creates two issues: high token overhead and weak visual grounding as long traces cause the model to attend increasingly to its own generated text rather than the image evidence. The question is not merely how to shorten outputs, but whether reasoning can be moved from the natural-language space into a denser structured trace space that preserves or improves correctness while using far fewer tokens.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper; it sits in post-training and reasoning-format design. Still, it has practical relevance for foundation-model training because it highlights that reasoning quality depends not only on model scale and test-time compute, but also on the representation language used for intermediate states. If the intermediate channel is too linguistic, capacity is wasted on phrasing rather than inference. The impact is moderate. \\texttt{<interrupt>}","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-18T01:04:53Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2609_21183.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.SD","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的是 onset interrupt；I2 表示窗口内全是相关音频、没有前置无关上下文时仍应触发，用来补救错过 onset 的情况；S1 表示无关音频保持沉默；S2 表示音频相关但历史中已经提醒过，需要保持沉默以避免重复通知。模型使用 Qwen2-Audio-7B，底层包含 Whisper-large-v3 音频编码器和 7B 语言模型，总规模约 8B。音频重采样到 16 kHz，转为 128 维 ISM 在该场景下有更好的 interrupt 表现，并展示了从 ESC-50 到 Epic-Sounds 的零样本迁移能力。一个有用的消融是 I2 训练：加入 sustained-relevance interruption 后，I2 recall 提升约 10 个百分点。这说明只训练 onset 触发不够，流式系统必须处理“已经进入事件中段才开始观察”的情况。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，预计 20–30 分钟。重点看任务定义和 Method 中四种状态的构造方式，尤其是 I2 与 S2，因为它们分别对应流式漏检补救和通知去重。实验部分建议看 proactive evaluation metrics、streaming protocol 和 I2 ablation。若关注 query-driven. Given a single natural-language watch-out intent, the model monitors a streaming audio input and decides whether to notify the user or stay silent. The target setting is explicit audio assistance, such as alerting Deaf or Hard of Hearing users when a requested sound occurs.","importance":"The work is not a pretraining contribution in the narrow sense, but it is useful for audio-language modeling interfaces. It shows that proactive behavior can be represented as ordinary autoregressive token prediction, without changing the audio encoder or the LLM backbone. The most relevant idea is to model notification history and de-duplication inside the language model objective, rather than as a separate rule-based layer.","technical_approach":"The proposed method, Interrupt and Silent Modeling, adds two special tokens to the LLM vocabulary: <interrupt> and <silent>. Four decision states are used to construct training data. I1 captures the onset of a relevant event after irrelevant audio. I2 captures a fully relevant window where the initial onset may have been missed. S1 is silence for irrelevant audio. S2 is silence for relevant audio that has already triggered a prior notification in the conversation history. The backbone is Qwen2-Audio-7B, with a Whisper-large-v3 audio encoder and a 7B language model, roughly 8B parameters in total. Audio is resampled to 16 kHz and encoded as 128-dimensional mel-spectrograms with 25 ms windows and 10 ms hop; after stride-2 pooling, the temporal resolution is about 40 ms per token. Training has two stages: reactive SFT for sound classification, followed by proactive SFT where interrupt examples target <interrupt> plus response text, and silent examples target <silent>.","experiments":"On ESC-50, the method reports 99.6% interrupt F1 and 100% de-duplication recall. This suggests that the two-token formulation is sufficient for clean, well-labeled audio events. On noisy Epic-Sounds kitchen audio, the paper reports stronger interrupt behavior and emphasizes zero-shot transfer from ESC-50-style training. A useful ablation is the I2 state: adding sustained-relevance training improves I2 recall by about 10 percentage points, indicating that onset-only supervision is inadequate for streaming monitoring.","reading_guide":"Skim in 20–30 minutes. wor","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-18T08:00:06Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2609_21449.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的是：如何把异构触觉传感器、不同机械手/夹爪形态、视觉观测与动作序列放进同一个可预测的世界动作模型里，并且让模型不仅把触觉当条件输入，而是真正去预测未来触觉状态，再用这些未来视觉+未来触觉表征来生成动作。难点有三层。第一是数据层，现有机器人数据集大多没有原生同步触觉，且不同触觉数据格式差异很大。第二是表示层，不同传感器的空间布局、量纲和接触语义不一致，直接拼接训练很难形成共享表征。第三是模型层，如何在统一架构里同时建模未来视频、未来触觉和动作，并让动作生成阶段能访问到前两者的预测表征，而不是退化成普通的条件策略学习。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的意义不在于机器人应用本身，而在于它给出了一种较完整的多模态世界建模路径：先做跨 embodiment 的统一表示，再做联合未来状态建模，最后让动作头从共享未来表征中读信息。这个思路和统一多模态 pretrain 的问题是同构的，尤其像视觉 token、音频 token、动作 token、触觉 token 这类异构序列如何对齐到共享 latent 空间。论文的贡献量级更像“面向机器人触觉世界模型的系统性整合”，不是单点算法突破。它最有启发的地方有两个：一是 Canonical Hand Model + 统一触觉 AE 这套跨传感器对齐方案，二是把 future tactile prediction 提升到与 future video prediction 同等地位，而不是只把触觉作为辅助条件。","technical_approach":"方法由三部分组成。第一部分是统一触觉表示。作者设计了一个受人手启发的 Canonical Hand Model，把不同 dexterous hand 和 gripper 的触觉观测映射到统一模板上，建立共享空间参考系。然后用 Unified Tactile Autoencoder 把对齐后的触觉输入编码到共同 latent 空间，目标是既保留局部受力结构，又让不同传感器数据可联合训练。这里的关键不是简单做 feature projection，而是先做几何/拓扑层面的 canonicalization，再做共享编码，这比直接学习 sensor-specific adapter 更适合跨 embodiment 训练。\n\n第二部分是世界动作触觉联合建模。ME-Dex-1.0 采用三专家 Mixture-of-Transformers：Video Expert 负责未来视频预测，Tactile Expert 负责未来触觉 latent 预测，Action Expert 负责动作 chunk 生成。三者不是完全独立，而是在中间层通过 H-Bridge shared attention 交换信息。这样 Action Expert 在去噪/生成动作时，可以访问未来视觉和未来触觉的中间表示。论文强调用 flow matching 来联合优化三路预测任务，这说明其生成过程更接近连续轨迹建模，而不是标准离散 token AR。和已有把触觉仅作为 conditioning signal 的方法相比，这里显式建模 tactile dynamics，因此动作生成不只是“看到当前接触”，而是利用“接触将如何演化”的预测。\n\n第三部分是数据补全。作者提出 Agentic Tactile Data Engine，在仿真中通过轨迹 replay 记录力传感器数据，为 RoboTwin 和 DexJoCo 这类原本缺触觉的数据集补齐同步 tactile trajectory。这个模块的价值在于把数据缺口补上，否则统一模型设计无法落地。它更像一个数据引擎，而不是学习算法，但对训练闭环是必要条件。","experiments":"实验覆盖 RoboTwin、DexJoCo、ManiFeel，以及两个真实平台：带 Paxini PX6AX 传感器的 LeRobot SO-101 和 Xynova Flex2 dexterous hands。论文主结论是：在平均 manipulation success 上，ME-Dex-1.0 相比对比方法在 gripper 和 dexterous hand 场景都更好，并且在双手任务以及 RoboTwin 的 Clean→Random 泛化设置下也有优势。全文给出的定性结论比较明确：联合未来视频+未来触觉预测优于只做视觉世界模型或只把触觉当条件输入；统一触觉表示有助于跨 embodiment 训练；补充的仿真触觉数据能提升模型泛化。\n\n从可读到的正文内容看，最值得关注的实验不是单个绝对分数，而是几类消融：1）去掉 Tactile Expert 或不预测 future tactile，性能会下降，说明触觉预测不是装饰项；2）不用 Canonical Hand Model / Unified Tactile AE 时，跨平台联合训练效果变差，说明异构触觉不能靠裸拼接解决；3）在 domain randomization 或 Clean-to-Random 评测中，触觉分支带来更稳的接触相关操作表现。当前提供的全文片段没有完整表格数字，因此无法可靠复述具体提升幅度、参数量和训练 token 规模；但从任务设置看，这篇论文更偏系统验证，重点是跨数据源、跨 embodiment、跨 sim2real 的一致收益，而不是在单一 benchmark 上追求大幅度数值领先。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，重点放在三处。第一看 Introduction 和方法总图，先确认作者到底解决的是“触觉作为条件”还是“触觉作为未来状态预测目标”，这是理解整篇工作的分水岭。第二看 Canonical Hand Model 与 Unified Tactile AE 的设计细节，尤其不同手型/夹爪如何映射到统一模板，这部分决定了方法能否跨 embodiment 扩展。第三看实验里的消融和 sim2real 结果，判断性能收益主要来自数据补全、表示对齐，还是联合预测架构本身。\n\n如果时间有限，建议按“图2 → 统一触觉表示章节 → 主结果表 → 消融表 → 真实机器人实验”这个顺序读，30–45 分钟足够建立判断。对做 LLM/pretrain 的读者，值得借鉴的是异构模态先 canonicalize 再共享编码的思路；不必在机器人任务细节上停留太久。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a concrete multimodal world-modeling problem: how to train a single predictive model over vision, action, and tactile signals when tactile observations come from heterogeneous sensors and different robot embodiments. The key point is not merely to condition on tactile input, but to predict future tactile states alongside future visual states, and then use both predicted futures to support action generation. The difficulty is threefold: missing tactile data in major robot datasets, incompatible tactile representations across grippers and dexterous hands, and the modeling challenge of coupling future video, future tactile dynamics, and action chunks in one architecture.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model research comes from the representation and training design rather than the robotics application itself. The paper is a useful case study in unified multimodal pretraining under severe modality heterogeneity: first align disparate sensors into a canonical space, then learn a shared latent representation, then jointly model future states and control outputs. The contribution is more system-level than algorithmically radical, but it is informative for anyone thinking about unified token spaces across modalities. Two ideas stand out: the canonicalization of heterogeneous tactile inputs before shared encoding, and the decision to elevate future tactile prediction to the same status as future video prediction instead of treating touch as a side-channel.","technical_approach":"$2f","experiments":"$30","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper. We hypothesize that long reasoning sequences shift attention away from the audio input. integrate evidence across multiple documents.","importance":"The work is relevant to the pretrain–posttrain interface. It argues that DR-RL provides uneven supervision: short successful trajectories produce clean positive signals, while long trajectories are more likely to contain repeated searches, noisy evidence, and confusion, and therefore contribute fewer useful successes. The proposed data loop turns agent rollouts into long-context QA training instances, making the same interaction traces useful for both agent learning and long-context reasoning. This is a more training-signal-oriented approach than merely increasing the context window.","technical_approach":"The method has two components. DR-to-Long converts Deepresearch rollouts into multi-document long-context QA. The original question is paired with expanded webpages from the trajectory; visited pages provide evidence, while retrieved but unused pages act as hard distractors. Successful rollouts can be converted directly because their final answers indicate that the collected context is sufficient. Failed rollouts are more challenging but may miss required evidence, so the method augments them with compact, high-value search snippets and webpage summaries from a successful rollout of the same question. This preserves the noise and distractors of failed trajectories while restoring answer sufficiency. On top of this data conversion, DLD-RL follows a three-stage loop: short DR-RL to generate traces, LongQA-RL on the converted long-context data, and then resumed DR-RL for agent performance.","experiments":"The paper analyzes 1,000 erroneous trajectories from existing DR models using a stronger model and attributes 61.6% of the errors to insufficient long-context understanding. DR-to-Long can generate 64K–128K-token training instances by controlling the number of expanded webpages, and it can scale to million-token instances without extra annotation. Experiments are run on two models initialized from Deepresearch-SFT checkpoints. Compared with continuing standard DR-RL alone, DLD-RL gives about 7.3% average relative improvement across three Deepresearch benchmarks and about 13.5% average relative improvement across three long-context benchmarks. The analysis also reports behavioral changes: after LongQA-RL, the agent uses the Visit tool more often to inspect full pages. Augmented failed-rollout data is reported to be more effective than data built only from successful rollouts.","reading_guide":"A practical skim takes about 20 minutes. 类数学题删除一个关键变量，形成与原题配对的欠规范问题；AbstentionBench 覆盖更广的拒答原因，包括上下文不足、意图不清、过期信息、错误前提、未知或主观问题等。第二步是训练干预：提出 SURE reward，用于 GRPO。该奖励把 outcome reward 与 process reward 结合起来。关键不是简单惩罚长度，而是惩罚模型在 CoT 已经识别出“关键信息缺失”之后继续推理。这样可以保留可解任务上的推理能力，同时压低不可解任务上的无效计算。","experiments":"实验包含人类基线和 LRM 对比。人类实验采用 2×2×3 设计：问题类型为可回答/不可回答，领域为数学任务和常识问题，任务重要性为高/default/低，并用积分和奖金调节激励。材料从两个领域、两类问题中各抽 6 个样本，共 24 个题目，并由作者筛掉不自然样本。模型评估覆盖 4B–32B 参数规模、六个模型家族；每个 benchmark 使用 500 个测试样本。主要发现是：人类能较准确地区分可回答与不可回答问题，并且不可回答任务的用时不超过可回答任务；LRM 则相反，在应当拒答的样本上经常生成更长 CoT。SURE 微调后，模型的拒答表现和拒答效率都有改善，同时保持可回答任务上的回答能力。提供内容中没有完整列出各模型的具体增益数字，精读时需要回到实验表格确认。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 20–30 分钟。优先看 Figure 1，那里集中展示了人类与 LRM 在拒答准确率和推理开销上的差异。然后看人类实验设计部分，确认 completion time 如何被当作 reasoning effort。再看模型评估和 SURE reward 定义，重点判断它与普通 length penalty 的区别。若关心 RL 训练信号，Section 6 和相关消融最值得细看；若关心评测可靠性，Appendix 中的 LLM judge prompt 和拒答判定规则需要检查。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a specific failure mode of large reasoning models: on underspecified or unanswerable tasks, they often fail to abstain and instead spend more tokens reasoning. The missing capability is not just refusal; it is early recognition that the problem lacks task-critical information.","importance":"The main relevance for pretraining-oriented research is about training signals for reasoning. Rewarding long chains of thought and final-answer accuracy can implicitly teach a model that more reasoning is always useful. That assumption breaks on unanswerable prompts. The paper points to a useful post-training interface: reward functions should model information sufficiency and computational cost, not only correctness and verbosity.","technical_approach":"The work first diagnoses the behavior using paired answerable and unanswerable tasks from QuestBench and broader abstention cases from AbstentionBench. QuestBench uses GSM-Q, where a key variable is removed from GSM-style math problems, creating underspecified prompts paired with answerable originals. AbstentionBench covers multiple reasons for abstention, including missing context, underspecified intent, stale data, false premises, unknown answers, and subjective questions. Outputs are labeled for abstention with an LLM judge. The intervention is SURE, a GRPO reward that combines an outcome reward with a process reward. The process term penalizes reasoning that continues after the chain of thought has already established that crucial information is missing. This differs from a generic length penalty: the target is not to make every answer short, but to stop after a sufficiency decision has been reached.","experiments":"The study includes a human reference experiment and model evaluations. The human experiment uses a 2×2×3 design: answerable vs. unanswerable questions, math vs. commonsense domains, and three levels of task importance implemented through points and bonus payments. The material contains 24 manually filtered items. Model evaluations cover six model families in the 4B–32B parameter range, with 500 test samples from each benchmark. The reported qualitative pattern is clear: humans identify both answerable and unanswerable cases accurately, and their effort on unanswerable tasks is upper-bounded by effort on answerable tasks. LRMs often do the opposite, producing longer CoTs on prompts where they should abstain. SURE fine-tuning improves abstention efficiency and performance while preserving answering ability. The provided content does not include the full per-model numerical deltas, so the result tables should be checked before citing exact gains.","reading_guide":"Skim in 20–30 minutes. tournament survival depth 作为置信度：越深轮次仍能支撑胜出的关键步骤，越可能是真正有用的局部行为；系统用 depth-aware weighting 和 intra-trajectory normalization，把轨迹级 advantage 分配到这些步骤。skill-level credit propagation 维护一个全局 skill memory，通过 group-level usage attribution 估计 skill utility。多次出现在关键成功步骤中的 skill 被提高权重，无效或过期 skill 被剪掉；后续 rollout 时，高 utility skill 会被检索出来作为策略先验，引导模型探索更有希望的解法。与普通 pairwise RL Despite recent advances in post-training methods, such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), their practical deployment remains impeded by training instability arising from the reliance on importance sampling.   We introduce Group Variance Policy Optimization (GVPO), a novel post-training method that integrates the analytical solution of KL-constrained r","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-18T07:44:54Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_21432.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"GVPO++要解决的是LLM后训练中基于组采样的RL方法不稳定的问题，尤其是GRPO依赖importance sampling ratio后可能带来的高方差和梯度爆炸。","importance":"这篇工作对预训练研究的间接价值在于，它把后训练中的RL目标、KL约束、参考策略和采样分布关系讲得更清楚。对于基座训练研究员，关键启发不是某个下游任务收益，而是训练信号如何被写成log-likelihood梯度的加权和，以及权重设计如何影响稳定性、策略偏移和熵。这个框架也和pretrain到posttrain的接口有关：如果后训练目标可以在不依赖高方差重要性采样的情况下稳定优化，那么预训练阶段保留多样性、参考模型选择、annealing后的策略可塑性都会更容易分析。","technical_approach":"方法从一个统一梯度形式出发：SFT、拒绝采样、GRPO等方法的梯度都可以看作对候选回答的log-likelihood梯度加权求和。正权重提升偏好回答的概率，负权重压低不偏好回答的概率，权重大小控制更新强度。GVPO把KL约束奖励最大化的解析最优解引入权重设计。困难在于解析形式里有partition function，需要对所有可能回答求期望，通常不可计算。作者利用一个条件绕开它：同一prompt组内分配给回答的权重和为零时，partition function在比较中抵消。基于这个条件，GVPO把每个回答的权重设为两种“中心距离”的差：一种来自当前策略和参考策略形成的隐式奖励，另一种来自真实奖励。作者给出两个解释。第一，GVPO梯度等价于一个均方误差目标的梯度，用来匹配隐式奖励中心距离和真实奖励中心距离。第二，GVPO损失可分解为组相对优势项、当前策略与参考策略的协方差约束项、当前策略方差项；分别对应收益提升、限制过度偏离、维持适度熵。相比GRPO，它避免直接使用不受控的importance sampling ratio；相比DPO，它声称最优解与KL约束奖励最大化的唯一最优解一致，并不依赖Bradley-Terry偏好模型的限制。","experiments":"给出的全文内容未包含完整实验设置、模型规模、数据集、训练步数或性能数字。可确认的结果主张包括两点：GVPO在理论上有唯一最优解，且该解与KL约束奖励最大化目标一致；GVPO允许更灵活的采样分布，在满足温和条件时仍保留理论保证，并避免GRPO式importance sampling导致的梯度不稳定。作者还把方法扩展到on-policy distillation，将reverse KL目标改写为KL正则化RL目标，并采用sequence-level优化，因此可用于tokenizer或词表不兼容的模型家族之间蒸馏。完整实验若可获得，建议重点检查训练曲线稳定性、reward提升与KL漂移、不同采样分布下的鲁棒性、与GRPO/DPO/RS/SFT的公平比较，以及跨tokenizer蒸馏是否真的受益于sequence-level目标。","reading_guide":"泛读建议用25到35分钟。先看统一梯度形式和零和权重条件，这是理解GVPO为何能绕开partition in LLM post-training with group-sampled RL methods, especially the high-variance and potentially exploding gradients caused by importance-sampling ratios in GRPO-style updates.","importance":"The main relevance to pretraining research is indirect but useful. The work clarifies the relation among RL objectives, KL constraints, reference policies, and sampling distributions in post-training. A useful point is that SFT, rejection sampling, GRPO, and related methods can be viewed as weighted sums of log-likelihood gradients. Once written this way, stability depends heavily on how response-level weights are designed. This connects to the pretrain-posttrain interface: stable KL-regularized updates make it easier to reason about policy plasticity after pretraining, the role of the reference model, and how much diversity should be preserved before reasoning-oriented training.","technical_approach":"$33","experiments":"The provided full text does not include complete experimental settings, model sizes, datasets, training budgets, or numerical results. The stated results are mainly theoretical: GVPO has a unique optimal solution matching the KL-constrained reward-maximization optimum, and it supports flexible sampling distributions under mild conditions while avoiding importance-sampling-induced instability. The method is also extended to on-policy distillation by reformulating reverse KL as a KL-regularized RL objective. Because the optimization is sequence-level, it can distill between model families with incompatible tokenizers or vocabularies. In the complete experiments, the key checks would be training stability, reward gain versus KL drift, robustness under different sampling distributions, fair comparison with GRPO/DPO/rejection sampling/SFT, and whether cross-tokenizer distillation benefits from the sequence-level formulation.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25 to 35 minutes. To address the gap whereby LLMs arrive at correct final answers through logically flawed intermediate reasoning chains, we propose LogicTrack, a neuro-symbolic framework that audits reasoning trajectories by auto-formalizing each reasoni","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-18T08:46:37Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_21492.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LO","cs.SC"],"summary":{"core_problem":"LogicTrack 解决的是 CoT 推理中“答案正确但过程不可靠”的问题。现有 outcome-based RL、DPO 或普通 PRM 主要看最终答案，最多用人工或 LLM judge 给过程打分，难以保证中间步骤在形式逻辑上有效。论文把问题推进到生成过程中：每一步推理刚生成出来，就尝试形式化并用自动定理证明器检查；一旦发现逻辑失败，就从错误步骤回退，而不是等完整答案生成完再做事后审计。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接贡献不在基础 LM 训练目标，而在 reasoning post-training 和过程监督信号质量。它提供了一个较硬的 step-level reward 来源：不是让另一个 LLM 判断推理是否合理，而是把自然语言步骤转成 solver 可验证的逻辑表达。这个方向对 R1-style 长链推理训练有启发：过程监督如果只依赖模型偏好，很容易学到风格、长度和模板；形式验证可以把一部分训练信号从“像不像好推理”收紧到“这一步能否从前提推出”。局限也清楚：适用面主要在可形式化的逻辑推理任务，auto-formalization 的错误会成为新的瓶颈。","technical_approach":"方法分三层。第一层是 step decomposition：把每个自然语言推理步骤拆成 premises、explanations 和 conclusions，显式区分当前步骤依赖了哪些前提、做了什么推断、得到了什么结论。第二层是 auto-formalization 与 solver verification：将这些片段转成符号逻辑规格，再交给自动定理证明器验证结论是否可由给定前提推出。第三层是 Solver-Based Backtracking Reward，即 SBR。SBR 不只看 solver 是否通过，还结合 premise fidelity，避免模型通过错误引用或篡改前提来得到形式上可证明的结论。推理时，如果某一步的 SBR 低于阈值，系统触发 backtracking，从该步骤重新生成后续推理。和 LogicReward、VeriCoT 这类事后验证方法相比，LogicTrack 的区别是 verification 被放进 generation loop，错误在传播前被截断。论文还把该框架扩展到 SFT：用真实模型生成的错误轨迹和 solver 验证后的修正轨迹构造训练数据，并引入专门的 <backtrack> token，让模型学习在推理中自发回退。","experiments":"论文声称在 8 个推理 benchmark、7 个模型上评估，结果同时覆盖最终答案正确率和中间步骤有效性。主要结论是 LogicTrack 在不同模型和任务上带来一致提升；backtracking search 的不同变体和消融实验支持 SBR 的泛化性；用 LogicTrack 构造的 self-backtracking SFT 数据也能提升模型在多 benchmark 上的推理表现。给定内容没有提供具体 benchmark 名称、模型规模、绝对分数和提升幅度，因此需要在实验表中核对三类数字：一是 final-answer accuracy 的提升是否来自 solver 过滤而非简单增加采样；二是 intermediate-step validity 的统计口径；三是 auto-formalization 失败率和 solver 调用成本。若这些开销很高，方法更适合高可靠场景或训练数据构造，而不是普通在线推理。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 25–35 分钟。优先看 Methodology 中 decomposition、formalization 和 SBR 的定义，重点确认 SBR 如何同时约束 premise fidelity 和 logical validity。其次看 数据构造部分：真实错误轨迹加形式验证修正，比合成注入错误更接近模型实际错误分布。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"LogicTrack targets a specific failure mode in chain-of-thought reasoning: a model may produce the correct final answer while using logically invalid intermediate steps. Most outcome-driven training methods optimize the final result, and process supervision often relies on humans or LLM judges. The paper moves verification into the generation loop. Each reasoning step is formalized and checked by a symbolic solver as soon as it is produced; if the step fails, generation backtracks from the failure point.","importance":"The paper is more relevant to reasoning post-training than to base-model pretraining, but it matters for the pretrain–posttrain interface because it studies the quality of dense reasoning signals. For long-CoT training, this is useful because weak process rewards often capture style, verbosity, or template adherence. Solver-based verification can anchor part of the training signal to whether a conclusion follows from its premises. The limitation is also clear: the method is most applicable to tasks that can be reliably formalized, and auto-formalization errors become a central failure mode.","technical_approach":"$34","experiments":"The paper reports evaluation on eight reasoning benchmarks and seven models. privileged","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-18T11:00:44Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_21619.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"Cal-OPD Standard OPD treats the token-level log-likelihood gap between a teacher and an on-policy student as a dense advantage signal. The paper argues that this gap is not a clean capability gap. It also contains teacher-side variation caused by contextual sensitivity. Privileged OPD can make the problem worse: adding hints, answers, or reference solutions shifts the teacher distribution, and the student may learn these shifts rather than better reasoning.","importance":"The work is directly relevant to reasoning post-training and useful for thinking about training-signal quality more broadly. Its main contribution is the formalization of Teacher Self-Deviation: the teacher is not an equally stable reference at every token and reasoning position. This helps explain why stronger teachers, richer privileged context, or denser token-level supervision can sometimes produce length expansion, stylistic drift, or weaker final performance. The method is a calibration layer over OPD. It does not require a new teacher or a separate reward model; it estimates the teacher’s own variability and removes that part from the distillation signal.","technical_approach":"In standard OPD, the student samples a trajectory, and each student-generated token is scored by the difference between teacher and student log-likelihood under the same prefix. Cal-OPD first estimates how much the teacher likelihood for the same token can move under contextual interventions while the problem, student prefix, and evaluated token remain fixed. This variability is called Teacher Self-Deviation. The method uses positive and negative privileged interventions as probes of upward and downward likelihood shifts. Importantly, the privileged information is not distilled into the student. It is used only to calibrate the teacher reference. Cal-OPD then subtracts the estimated self-deviation region from the original teacher–student discrepancy. If the student likelihood lies within the estimated teacher-variation region, the advantage is set to zero; only residual discrepancy outside the region is optimized.","experiments":"The paper evaluates the method on mathematical reasoning benchmarks across model scales, comparing against standard OPD, privileged OPD, and related variants. The central empirical result is that Cal-OPD keeps only about 52–65% of the original teacher–student discrepancy for optimization while consistently outperforming OPD baselines. It also reduces the response-length expansion observed in standard and privileged OPD. outputs","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-18T12:13:24Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_21677.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文处理的是大推理模型的遗忘问题：目标内容不只会出现在最终答案里，也会在 chain-of-thought 轨迹中提前泄露。现有 unlearning 目标多是在压低目标内容概率、鼓励拒答，或重定向表示，但没有规定“忘掉之后应该如何继续生成”。结果是模型可能不泄露原始事实，却生成伪造替代事实、重复文本、空 reasoning、错误的 </think> 边界，或者最终答案安全但推理过程仍含有危险信息。","importance":"对预训练和 post-train token，让冻结模型对齐到这些目标轨迹；最后通过 answer-reasoning distillation 把这种行为蒸馏进模型参数，使部署时不再依赖额外 prompt 或运行时 token。与 GA 类方法相比，它不是单纯压低 protected token；与 preference/refusal-only 方法相比，它约束了 reasoning 和 answer 两个通道；与表示重定向方法相比，它避免过度破坏自回归解码状态。论文还提出 Natural Forgetting Response Score，评估结构稳定性、流畅性、以及是否出现无依据替代内容，用来补充 CFE、拒答率、安全率等泄露导向指标。","experiments":"实验覆盖隐私遗忘和有害内容遗忘两个设置。文中机制分析以 R-TOFU 1% 和 相关性很弱，说明最终拒答和中间推理没有充分耦合；R2MU 更强地压制泄露，但会放大解码漂移，出现重复、空 trace、边界损坏等结构问题。GUARD 在这些场景中同时改善遗忘强度、生成质量、效用保持和推理能力。给定内容未包含完整实验表，因此不宜补写具体百分比或绝对分数。","reading_guide":"泛读建议用 20–30 分钟。先看 Section 3 的失败模式诊断，尤其是 token 定义和实验表，核对它是否能区分“不泄露但胡编”和“自然拒答”。如果只关心预训练研究，可把它作为 reasoning trajectory supervision 和 unlearning objective 设计的案例来读。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses unlearning for large reasoning models when the protected content can appear in the chain-of-thought before the final answer. Existing objectives mostly suppress target likelihood, encourage refusal-like outputs, or redirect representations, but they do not define the replacement trajectory after forgetting. This leaves the autoregressive decoder under-specified and produces fabricated substitutes, malformed reasoning boundaries, repetition, empty traces, or safe-looking final answers with unsafe intermediate reasoning.","importance":"The main value is its reframing of unlearning as trajectory replacement rather than target removal. For pretraining and post-training research, the useful lesson is that lowering the probability of forbidden tokens is not enough for reasoning models; the model also needs a stable continuation policy after the original trajectory has been removed. This is relevant to continual pretraining, safety editing, and CoT-visible deployments. The contribution is methodological and evaluative rather than a new foundation-model training paradigm.","technical_approach":"$35","experiments":"The experiments cover privacy-oriented forgetting and harmful-content forgetting.
